51. Just as the flame of a lamp, extinguished of oil, becomes conserved as an element
within tbe wick, similarly tbe mind of an accomplished yogi, upon extinguisbing
bis association witb tbe matériái world, becomes witbdrawn from external affairs
and becomes focused on the Lord within his heart. (3.28.35)
52. Just as a devotee of tbe sun-god can see tbe sun-god as be is even from tbis planet,
even though devoid of eyesight, similarly Lord's devotee can see tbe Lord witbin
himself due to bis premá, even tbougb be is not on the levél of a jnáni. (3.9.42 P)
53. Just as witbout the sun, one's eyes has no value, similarly witbout tbe ligbt of tbe
Supreme Lord, or witbout tbe divine grace of tbe spiritual master, one cannot see
things as they are. (3.25.9 P)
54. a) Just as tbe eyes are naturally connected to the sun, tbe jnáni by dint of bis jnána
is naturally associated witb Brahman. But just as tbe eyes may not appreciate tbe
potency of the sun, jnáni because of bis lack of affection for Brahman, cannot
utilize His mercy, and remain everlastingly in máyá.
55. b) However, just as the otber limbs of tbe body, witbout tbe same affinity as that of
tbe eyes for tbe sun can appreciate the warmtb of sun, devotees witb or witbout
jnána can appreciate the Lord's mercy and release themselves from matériái life.
(3.9.42 P)
Offences in bhakti:
56. Offences against devotees engaged in missionary activities causes one's devotion
become as barren as an alkaline field, wbere there is no strength to produce. (3.9.12
P)
57. Lord Caitanya said that an offense to a devotee is just like a mad elephant run loose;
when a mad elephant enters a garden, it tramples all tbe plants. Similarly, an
offense unto tbe feet of a pure devotee murders one's position in devotional service.
(3.15.39 P)
Instructions on process of bhakti:
58. Just as a liver patient may understand tbe existence of milk in a cow, altbougb be
takes only ber urine as treatment, a person may understand from scripture tbe
existence of an ocean of milk altbougb be may only experience tbe salt water
ocean. (3.2.8 P)
59. It is said that a noble man accepts tbe good qualities of a person of doubtful
character, just as a person accepts nectar from a stock of poison. (3.2.23 P)
60. Tbe path of spiritual life is stated in tbe Vedas to be like tbe edge of a sharpened
razor. A little inattentiveness may at once create bavoc and bloodsbed. (3.9.24 P)
61. Sense gratification is just like salt. One cannot take too much or too little, but one
must take somé salt in order to make one's foodstuff palatable. (3.15.8 P)
62. Just as a spot of white leprosy on any part of tbe body poUutes all of the skin,
similarly a wrong act committed by a servant leads people in generál to blame bis
master. (3.16.5)
Jnána - specific realizations:
63. Just as kindling sticks rubbed togetber produce tbe very fire that consumes them,
similarly the jnáni's sádbana applies tbe subtle body to awaken átma-jnána in bis
beart, wbicb destroys tbe very source of bis knowiedge - bis subtle body. (3.27.23
V)
64. Just as gold, when put into fire and fanned witb air, becomes free from all
impurities, similarly tbe minds of yogis wbo practice breathing exercises become
purified, being freed from all mentái disturbances. (3.28.10)
65. Just as there is no benefit in busking the skin of an empty paddy as tbe rice is
already gone, there is no meaning in simply speculating about tbe Lord as in tbis
way one cannot know tbe Lord and become freed from matériái bondage. (3.27.20
P)
A B O U T T H E JÍVA
Jiva as átmá:
66. Just as a person in a dream sees bis head cut off, altbougb bis bead is still intact,
the jiva's apparent loss of self-identity is illusory, for be can never lose bis
constitutional knowiedge and bliss. (3.7.10 P)
67. Just as tbe reflection of the moon on tbe water appears to quiver, but tbe moon in
tbe sky is fixed. Similarly tbe living entities appear to be tainted by matériái
qualities like illusion, lamentation and miseries, but in tbe pure soul such qualities
are completely absent. (3.7.11 P)
68. Just as fire is exhibited differently in different forms of wood, similarly tbe soul in
tbe universal body, altbougb of tbe same quality, is different from tbe soul in tbe
